# Skills · D · part 88

799 entries, design-it-twice to design-me. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **design-it-twice**: Design two different high-level solutions for one refined feature or issue, or for one of its tasks, based on the existing code. The user chooses one, and the skill records the choice and updates the tasks. ([alenakatkova/idea2pr](https://github.com/alenakatkova/idea2pr/tree/HEAD/skills/design-it-twice))
- **design-it-twice**: Generate at least two fundamentally different designs and compare them before implementing. Use when the design space is genuinely open — a new module, API, data structure, or service where several shapes would plausibly work — or when the user asks to "redesign", "rethink", or "explore alternatives". Committing to the first design that comes to mind is a common source of mediocre software. ([andrezzoid/config](https://github.com/andrezzoid/config/tree/HEAD/dotfiles/.agents/skills/design-it-twice))
- **design-it-twice**: Generate two opposing designs in parallel via sub-agents with deliberately conflicting mandates, then compare on leverage, locality, and seam placement before picking. Use during brainstorm, grill, or improve-architecture whenever a shape-defining design choice has two defensible answers. Adapted from mattpocock/skills improve-codebase-architecture INTERFACE-DESIGN.md. ([devnoo/np-skills](https://github.com/devnoo/np-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/design-it-twice))
- **design-it-twice**: Before an architecture recommendation, draft two or three radically different interfaces for the same module with usage examples, compare depth, leverage, locality; pick. Writes work/arch/&lt;slug&gt;.md. ([hellojuste/forge](https://github.com/hellojuste/forge/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-it-twice))
- **design-it-twice**: Resolve a material design fork by parallel-divergent fan-out — spawn 3 designer sub-agents with deliberately different binding constraints, then synthesize a comparative analysis with a recommendation. Use when a P0 fork from /architecture-fit (or any planning skill) has 2+ defensible answers and the cost of being wrong is high relative to the cost of fanning out. Writes one comparative-design document under reports/design-it-twice/scan-&lt;TS&gt;/; does NOT author the ADR — /decide is the downstream step. ([KhurrumMahmood/senior-vibe-engineer](https://github.com/KhurrumMahmood/senior-vibe-engineer/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-it-twice))
- **design-it-twice**: Facilitate design exploration for a NEW module, API, or major architectural decision. Use when starting a design (new module, new interface/API, decomposition choice) — the first idea is rarely optimal. Sketch 2-3 fundamentally different approaches, list pros/cons of each, then pick or combine. This is a facilitate skill, not a review skill. ([phamngocdo/aposd-skills](https://github.com/phamngocdo/aposd-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/design-it-twice))
- **design-it-twice**: Use any time anything needs to be designed — any design or architecture decision, code or not: APIs, data models, algorithms, module boundaries, schemas, protocols, naming/conceptual structure, system shape. Generates genuinely independent candidate designs via parallel sub-agents given decorrelated framings, judges them adversarially, and synthesizes a winner. Surfaces the design-it-twice discipline whenever a decision is being made instead of leaving it buried. ([pterror/annotated-law](https://github.com/pterror/annotated-law/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-it-twice))
- **design-it-twice**: Generate 3+ radically different designs for an interface, each optimized for a different design constraint (minimize interface, maximize flexibility, optimize common caller, ports & adapters). Compare them by depth, locality, and seam placement; produce an opinionated recommendation. Use when the right interface shape isn't obvious — inside /deepen for alternative interface exploration, or anywhere else committing to one design without seeing alternatives would be premature. ([saaga0h/themis](https://github.com/saaga0h/themis/tree/HEAD/skills/design-it-twice))
- **design-it-twice**: Invoke before committing to a significant interface: new module, service boundary, public API, or library contract. Also when someone says the design is obvious or a review only tweaks names. Sketch two or more radically different designs and compare before implementing. ([StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market](https://github.com/StevenACoffman/steve-skill-market/tree/HEAD/plugins/software-design/skills/design-it-twice))

- **design-iterate**: One iteration of the showcase design-improvement loop - render the site locally, screenshot desktop + mobile, critique against the taste rubric, fix the top findings, verify the build, commit and push. Use via /loop for continuous improvement, or invoke once for a single pass. Usage: /design-iterate [focus-hint] ([0xSoftBoi/suwappubot](https://github.com/0xSoftBoi/suwappubot/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/design-iterate) · ★4)
- **design-iterate**: Two modes. `pick` (attended) — generate 2–3 style variants at the correct design-system layer (leaf scoped styles, shared wrapper, or design tokens), screenshot via Playwright MCP, present side-by-side so the user picks. `refine` (unattended, for /redesign Phase 6 + /art-direct build) — render the approved design, self-critique the PIXELS against the caller's rubric, fix the weakest lens (depth/motion/modern/perf/i18n), re-render, loop up to $MAX_REFINE rounds until it clears the bar. Honors $SCOPE_TIER so the same affordance is not duplicated across pages. Invoke for "try a few variants" / "… ([adnanmokhtar/refract](https://github.com/adnanmokhtar/refract/tree/HEAD/templates/packs/ui-ux/skills/design-iterate) · ★2)
- **design-iterate**: Use this skill to polish a rendered prototype against the design-qa rubric. Scores the prototype, makes one targeted fix per round, keeps or reverts based on score. Based on the autoresearch method, applied to prototypes instead of skills. Loaded by /design-kit:design-iterate. ([anindhitavidia/design-agent-kit](https://github.com/anindhitavidia/design-agent-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/core/skills/design-iterate) · ★2)
- **design-iterate**: Claude-Design-style visual iteration loop — render the running UI, screenshot at 375/768/1440, critique against docs/design/tokens.json and design principles, apply fixes, re-capture until clean (cap 3 iterations). Also extracts a token baseline from an existing codebase (--sync) and audits real logged-in browsers (--real). NOT a one-pass review — use /ux --review for findings-only; NOT functional conformance — use /ui-verify. ([bpmforge/attest](https://github.com/bpmforge/attest/tree/HEAD/skills/design-iterate) · ★2)
- **design-iterate**: Iteration loop driver for brand design. Calls design-board for N variants, renders each at mobile, tablet and desktop via preview-canvas + Claude Preview MCP, presents screenshots to the client via AskUserQuestion, captures feedback, refines, and repeats until a variant is approved. Use to design a brand, iterate on a design, or get variants to pick from, when a remotion-brand-research dossier needs visual directions, or when an existing brand identity needs a refresh. ([CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops](https://github.com/CleanExpo/Pi-Dev-Ops/tree/HEAD/skills/design-iterate) · ★1)
- **design-iterate**: Use when polishing or reviewing a UI screen on iOS, Android or the web: capture before and after screenshots on the device or browser, review, fix, and capture again. Not for backend or non-visual tasks. Triggers - UI polish, design review, screenshot, spacing, alignment, contrast, touch target, dark mode, reduced motion. ([filippkowalski/kmp-skills-by-filip](https://github.com/filippkowalski/kmp-skills-by-filip/tree/HEAD/skills/design-iterate) · ★1)
- **design-iterate**: Design playbook for web, Android and iOS. Improves, audits, polishes, redesigns or extends UI, a design system or brand assets such as a logo, app icon or favicon. Use when asked to improve or audit a logo or icon, make a page look better or more modern, tweak a header, colours, fonts or spacing, design a new screen or refresh a brand, and for any change to something several screens share, such as making all buttons pill-shaped, giving prices or titles a different colour, size or weight, or changing the accent colour, the base font or the corner radius. Keeps the project's DESIGN.md contract,… ([Mark-Hine/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/Mark-Hine/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/antigravity/plugins/design-kit/skills/design-iterate) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **design-iterate**: Implement → screenshot → critique → fix loop for web UI. Use this skill whenever building or reworking any user-facing screen, page, section, or component — landing pages, marketing sites, dashboards, forms — even when the user only says "make a hero section" or "build the pricing page" and never mentions design quality, review, or iteration. Without this loop the model never sees its own output and ships broken spacing and generic layouts. ([uta-a/design-iterate](https://github.com/uta-a/design-iterate/tree/HEAD/plugins/design-iterate/skills/design-iterate))
- **design-iterate-in-place**: Use when iterating an existing design in place with A/B variants, an editable artboard, and anti-slop moodboards instead of generating from zero. Triggers on "iterate design", "design variants", "artboard edit", "click to edit", "anti-slop". Non-triggers: blank-page prototype generation (use cloud-design-prototyping), generic UI polish rules (use frontend-design-direction). Outcome: variant set against the existing system plus an edited winner ready to implement. ([majinmagros/magros.ai-skills](https://github.com/majinmagros/magros.ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/design-iterate-in-place) · ★2)
- **design-iteration**: Structures the feedback loop between making and learning — prototyping, testing, and convergence toward fit. Triggers: 'prototype and test', 'iterate on the design', 'how do we test this', 'get feedback on the design', 'what should we prototype', 'design feedback loop', 'test and learn', 'are we converging'. ([rondoflow/rondoflow](https://github.com/rondoflow/rondoflow/tree/HEAD/packages/catalog/content/skills/design-iteration) · ★33)
- **design-iteration**: One-fix-at-a-time, approval-gated design fix recovery. Given a checklist of fixes (handover doc, CHECKLIST.md, or inline list), applies each individually, presents a diff, and waits for an explicit APPROVE / REJECT / NO CHANGES decision before committing. Trigger phrases: /design-iteration, "go through the checklist one by one", "fix these one at a time", "bucket recovery", "design fix pass". ([gaia-research/gaia-skill-tree](https://github.com/gaia-research/gaia-skill-tree/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/design-iteration) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **design-iteration**: Refine interfaces through visual feedback rounds against an append-only liked/rejected ledger. ([vasyapym/vasyapym.github.io](https://github.com/vasyapym/vasyapym.github.io/tree/HEAD/skills/productivity/design-iteration) · ★20)
- **design-iteration**: Improve design mockups from critique (design-review, user studies, a multi-perspective council, a UI lint pass, or raw notes): triage keep/change/kill, produce a new version with a per-change rationale trail so the next test can measure improvement. If no critique exists yet, runs the full test-iterate-test loop (review + studies + lint) around the mockup. Reaches for a design system if one exists. Use when: run design-iteration on this mockup, iterate on these designs, revise the mockups from feedback, incorporate the review, next design iteration, close the loop on design feedback. ([exiao/pm-skills](https://github.com/exiao/pm-skills/tree/HEAD/design/design-iteration) · ★11)
- **design-iteration**: Revise existing designs from critique or user feedback, preserving the design system and tracing keep/change/kill decisions to evidence. Use for design iteration, incorporating reviews, or an explicitly requested test-revise-retest loop. ([exiao/product-factory](https://github.com/exiao/product-factory/tree/HEAD/skills/design-iteration) · ★7)
- **design-iteration**: Design-iteration passes that an agent runs over its own code before it reports a structural change as done. Use after adding or splitting a component, changing a contract or public API, rewriting a module, or moving responsibility between modules, and before listing a design smell as an accepted cost. Covers the pass loop, the question bank, and when to apply versus propose. ([joinava/cache](https://github.com/joinava/cache/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/design-iteration))
- **design-iteration**: Refine and improve designs based on feedback, testing, and evolving requirements through iterative cycles.
